{"parentUuid":"fbbdc57e-66dd-4076-b43d-7eec55c93e00","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KqFJwvpUa9UXKuFjneaULz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Phone-as-gesture-trackpad landmark stream (Phase 9, W2-I). The PHONE runs its OWN on-device hand-\n2\tlandmark detection and streams tiny `List[Hand]` batches (landmark DATA, never owner pixels) to the PC\n3\tover the owner's own tunnel; the PC feeds them through the EXISTING gesture pipeline. This slice streams\n4\tinto a LOCAL owner-armed session ONLY — the owner arms at the PC (`run_gesture(auto_arm=False, gate=<armed>)`)\n5\tand the phone supplies landmarks. The device-signed REMOTE arm is a SEPARATE, later-authorized slice and\n6\tis deliberately NOT built here.\n7\t\n8\tDesign (mirrors the on-box components; NO dependency on `sigil/bridge/` — an inbound batch iterable is\n9\tINJECTED, so there is no socket/import coupling here):\n10\t\n11\t  • `decode_hand_batch(obj) -> List[Hand]` — parse+VALIDATE the compact wire form; on ANY malformation\n12\t    return an HONEST `[]` (never a fabricated hand), exactly as `OnnxHandLandmarker.detect` returns `[]`\n13\t    on failure.\n14\t  • `RemoteLandmarkSource` — the frame source: wraps the injected inbound feed, drops foreign-session /\n15\t    replayed / reordered / stale batches, and yields ONE token (the decoded `List[Hand]`) per ACCEPTED\n16\t    batch. It tracks only a single last-accepted `seq` (a bounded int — no unbounded buffer), and because\n17\t    `seq` is strictly monotonic an out-of-order (older) batch arriving after a newer one is dropped\n18\t    (drop-to-latest).\n19\t  • `RemoteLandmarker` — a `LandmarkModel` with `egresses = False` (HONEST: it uploads NO owner pixels;\n20\t    the phone already did the inference and sends inbound landmark DATA). `detect(token)` returns the\n21\t    token's hands, ignoring any image.\n22\t\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\timport math\n26\timport time\n27\tfrom typing import Iterable, Iterator, List, Optional\n28\t\n29\tfrom .types import Hand\n30\t\n31\tWIRE_TYPE = \"hand_batch\"\n32\tMAX_HANDS = 2\n33\tN_LANDMARKS = 21\n34\t_COORD_CAP = 8.0          # sane bound: reject NaN/inf/garbage coordinates (normalized hands live near [0,1])\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _is_number(v) -> bool:\n38\t    # a real, finite number — bools are ints in Python, so exclude them explicitly\n39\t    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(float(v))\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef _valid_coord(v) -> bool:\n43\t    return _is_number(v) and abs(float(v)) <= _COORD_CAP\n44\t\n45\t\n46\tdef decode_hand_batch(obj) -> List[Hand]:\n47\t    \"\"\"Decode a compact wire batch to `List[Hand]`, VALIDATING strictly. Wire form:\n48\t\n49\t        {\"t\":\"hand_batch\",\"session_id\":str,\"seq\":int,\"ts\":float,\n50\t         \"hands\":[ {\"l\":[[x,y,z] × 21], \"h\":\"R\"|\"L\", \"s\":score}, ... ≤ 2 ]}\n51\t\n52\t    Rules (any violation ⇒ return `[]`, honest-empty — never fabricate):\n53\t      • obj is a dict tagged `\"t\":\"hand_batch\"`;\n54\t      • `hands` is a list of AT MOST 2 hands;\n55\t      • each hand has EXACTLY 21 landmarks, each a 3-tuple of finite, sane (|v|≤8) floats;\n56\t      • handedness `h` ∈ {\"R\",\"L\"} (→ \"Right\"/\"Left\"), defaulting \"R\" only when the key is absent;\n57\t      • score `s` a finite float in [0,1], defaulting 1.0 only when the key is absent.\n58\t    \"\"\"\n59\t    try:\n60\t        if not isinstance(obj, dict) or obj.get(\"t\") != WIRE_TYPE:\n61\t            return []\n62\t        raw_hands = obj.get(\"hands\")\n63\t        if not isinstance(raw_hands, list) or len(raw_hands) > MAX_HANDS:\n64\t            return []\n65\t        out: List[Hand] = []\n66\t        for rh in raw_hands:\n67\t            if not isinstance(rh, dict):\n68\t                return []\n69\t            lms = rh.get(\"l\")\n70\t            if not isinstance(lms, (list, tuple)) or len(lms) != N_LANDMARKS:\n71\t                return []\n72\t            pts: List[tuple] = []\n73\t            for p in lms:\n74\t                if not isinstance(p, (list, tuple)) or len(p) != 3:\n75\t                    return []\n76\t                if not (_valid_coord(p[0]) and _valid_coord(p[1]) and _valid_coord(p[2])):\n77\t                    return []\n78\t                pts.append((float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(p[2])))\n79\t            h = rh.get(\"h\", \"R\")\n80\t            if h not in (\"R\", \"L\"):\n81\t                return []\n82\t            handed = \"Right\" if h == \"R\" else \"Left\"\n83\t            s = rh.get(\"s\", 1.0)\n84\t            if not _is_number(s) or not (0.0 <= float(s) <= 1.0):\n85\t                return []\n86\t            out.append(Hand(landmarks=tuple(pts), handedness=handed, score=float(s)))\n87\t        return out\n88\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — ANY malformation ⇒ honest empty (mirror OnnxHandLandmarker.detect)\n89\t        return []\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tclass ScriptedRemoteSource:\n93\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double — an injected inbound feed of raw wire dicts (no socket, no `bridge`).\n94\t    Mirrors `perception.camera_stream.ScriptedFrameSource`: replays a fixed list of batch objects.\"\"\"\n95\t\n96\t    def __init__(self, batches: Iterable):\n97\t        self._batches = list(batches)\n98\t\n99\t    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator:\n100\t        return iter(self._batches)\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tclass RemoteLandmarkSource:\n104\t    \"\"\"Frame source over an INJECTED inbound batch feed, bound to the LIVE local session.\n105\t\n106\t    `frames()` yields ONE token (the decoded `List[Hand]`) per ACCEPTED batch. A batch is REJECTED\n107\t    (dropped, never fabricated) when its `session_id` ≠ the live session, its `seq` ≤ the last accepted\n108\t    `seq` (duplicate / reorder / replay), or — if a freshness window is set — its `ts` is outside it.\n109\t    The paired `RemoteLandmarker` returns the yielded token, so the token IS the landmark payload.\"\"\"\n110\t\n111\t    def __init__(self, inbound: Iterable, session_id: str, *,\n112\t                 freshness_seconds: Optional[float] = None, now=time.time):\n113\t        self._inbound = inbound\n114\t        self.session_id = session_id\n115\t        self._freshness = freshness_seconds\n116\t        self._now = now\n117\t        self._last_seq: Optional[int] = None   # bounded: a single int, never an unbounded accepted-set\n118\t\n119\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n120\t        return True\n121\t\n122\t    def _accept(self, obj) -> Optional[List[Hand]]:\n123\t        \"\"\"Return the decoded hands for an ACCEPTED batch, or None if the batch is rejected.\"\"\"\n124\t        if not isinstance(obj, dict) or obj.get(\"t\") != WIRE_TYPE:\n125\t            return None\n126\t        if obj.get(\"session_id\") != self.session_id:            # foreign session → drop\n127\t            return None\n128\t        seq = obj.get(\"seq\")\n129\t        if not isinstance(seq, int) or isinstance(seq, bool):   # seq must be a real int\n130\t            return None\n131\t        if self._last_seq is not None and seq <= self._last_seq:   # replay / reorder / duplicate → drop\n132\t            return None\n133\t        if self._freshness is not None:                          # optional freshness window\n134\t            ts = obj.get(\"ts\")\n135\t            if not _is_number(ts) or abs(self._now() - float(ts)) > self._freshness:\n136\t                return None\n137\t        hands = decode_hand_batch(obj)   # may be [] (honest) — a valid envelope with malformed hands\n138\t        self._last_seq = seq             # advance ONLY once the batch is accepted at the envelope level\n139\t        return hands\n140\t\n141\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[List[Hand]]:\n142\t        for obj in self._inbound:\n143\t            hands = self._accept(obj)\n144\t            if hands is None:            # rejected — note: [] is ACCEPTED (honest no-hand), None is not\n145\t                continue\n146\t            yield hands\n147\t\n148\t\n149\tclass RemoteLandmarker:\n150\t    \"\"\"A `LandmarkModel` fed by the phone's OWN on-device inference. `egresses = False` is HONEST —\n151\t    it uploads NO owner pixels; the inbound stream is landmark DATA over the owner's own tunnel. Because\n152\t    `egresses is False`, the Wave-1 fail-closed egress gate in `run_gesture` PASSES it (reconciliation:\n153\t    that gate refuses a model that would EGRESS owner imagery; this one egresses nothing).\"\"\"\n154\t\n155\t    egresses = False\n156\t    source_kind = \"remote_device_stream\"\n157\t\n158\t    def detect(self, token) -> List[Hand]:\n159\t        # `token` is the decoded List[Hand] yielded by RemoteLandmarkSource.frames(); return it as-is,\n160\t        # ignoring any image (the phone already produced these landmarks). Honest-empty otherwise.\n161\t        if isinstance(token, list):\n162\t            return token\n163\t        return []\n164\t"}]},"uuid":"20747410-fe70-4a38-afc7-709353d9389b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:28.297Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fbbdc57e-66dd-4076-b43d-7eec55c93e00","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"be798de4-7934-4ae7-b778-36a0e6414f42","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014dFHhyUxiCpdM5JzGnSEiv","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The live gesture-control daemon (Phase 8, WS-F F7) — camera stream → landmarks → classify → FSM →\n2\tSessionGate, mirroring `voice/run.py run_mic`. Arms the session on start (indicator lit), disarms in a\n3\t`finally` (indicator cleared even on crash). Graceful fallbacks; components are injectable so the\n4\twhole loop runs deterministically on scripted doubles with no camera/model/OS input.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline\n10\tfrom .session import SessionGate\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, classifier=None,\n14\t                backend=None, pipeline=None, gate=None, max_frames: Optional[int] = None,\n15\t                auto_arm: bool = True) -> int:\n16\t    \"\"\"Run the loop; returns the number of frames processed. Injectable for tests.\n17\t\n18\t    `auto_arm=True` (default) is the camera path: owner-arm the session locally on start. `auto_arm=False`\n19\t    is for a caller that passes an ALREADY-ARMED `gate` — e.g. a LOCAL owner-armed session consuming a\n20\t    `remote.RemoteLandmarkSource` (owner arms at the PC; the phone streams landmarks in). This adds NO\n21\t    device-arm path; the owner still arms locally, and every other guarantee (egress gate, Layer-1\n22\t    session, Layer-2 tier gate, disarm-in-finally) is unchanged.\"\"\"\n23\t    if store is None:\n24\t        from ..spine.store import SpineStore\n25\t        store = SpineStore()\n26\t    if source is None:\n27\t        from ..perception.camera_stream import CameraStreamSource\n28\t        source = CameraStreamSource()\n29\t    if landmarker is None:\n30\t        from .landmark import OnnxHandLandmarker\n31\t        landmarker = OnnxHandLandmarker()\n32\t    # STRICT fail-closed egress gate (Phase 9 W2-H): this loop injects HID off a live camera stream,\n33\t    # so it refuses UNLESS the landmarker is EXPLICITLY on-box (`egresses is False`) — stricter than\n34\t    # perceive's default-safe `getattr(..., \"egresses\", False)` form, because an unset/unknown flag\n35\t    # here would mean streaming owner PIXELS off-box while driving the pointer. This gate refuses a\n36\t    # model that would EGRESS owner imagery; a future `RemoteLandmarker` with `egresses=False` (an\n37\t    # authorized INBOUND landmark-DATA stream over the owner's own tunnel) is a DISTINCT,\n38\t    # separately-authorized thing — it egresses nothing and passes this gate.\n39\t    if getattr(landmarker, \"egresses\", True) is not False:\n40\t        store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n41\t                     payload={\"signal\": \"gesture.refused\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n42\t                              \"reason\": \"landmark model would egress owner imagery — the gesture \"\n43\t                                        \"loop is on-box only\"})\n44\t        return 0  # inject NOTHING; never armed\n45\t    if classifier is None:\n46\t        from .features import RuleClassifier\n47\t        classifier = RuleClassifier()\n48\t    if backend is None:\n49\t        from ..platform import input_backend\n50\t        backend = input_backend()\n51\t    pipe = pipeline or GesturePipeline()\n52\t    g = gate or SessionGate(store, backend, owner_key=owner_key)\n53\t    if auto_arm:\n54\t        g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)   # camera path: owner-arm locally. When False, the caller's gate is\n55\t        # already armed (a LOCAL owner-armed session) and we consume it without a second arm.\n56\t    n = 0\n57\t    try:\n58\t        for frame in source.frames():\n59\t            reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n60\t            intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n61\t            if intent is not None:\n62\t                g.handle(intent)\n63\t            n += 1\n64\t            if max_frames is not None and n >= max_frames:\n65\t                break\n66\t    finally:\n67\t        g.disarm()\n68\t    return n\n69\t"}]},"uuid":"cc7f1c5a-970c-4f20-b005-f32e47cbd131","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:33.500Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"be798de4-7934-4ae7-b778-36a0e6414f42","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"eb963917-1954-4fb8-9051-497aa57250c6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011sCjR2bRG8nwZnScnUGLYn","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"2202f2f4-53cf-46df-81f1-fe6a3647ba21","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:33.959Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"eb963917-1954-4fb8-9051-497aa57250c6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"7cff2419-a1cb-4b73-9346-c433db5f54de","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAUMeVuSGL1QdXcTfVPZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014ofQ9oMGpBnYLAC5ip5wY4","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4256,"cache_read_input_tokens":53353,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4256,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUMcqwErU6EPXHTumrh","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"189cd6be-2d6b-4750-9aca-3675a58a4a5f","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:39.336Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"189cd6be-2d6b-4750-9aca-3675a58a4a5f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014ofQ9oMGpBnYLAC5ip5wY4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"ca4126e5-3b62-462c-a3a7-89aae0acde53","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:39.356Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"189cd6be-2d6b-4750-9aca-3675a58a4a5f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Signing helpers are\n5\tprovisioning-only; the runtime only ever verifies. The sole change from the source is a\n6\tlocal `IntegrityError` in place of the framework's `EntitlementError` (decoupling).\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\timport base64\n11\timport binascii\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n13\t\n14\tfrom cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature\n15\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (\n16\t    Ed25519PrivateKey,\n17\t    Ed25519PublicKey,\n18\t)\n19\tfrom cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import (\n20\t    Encoding,\n21\t    NoEncryption,\n22\t    PrivateFormat,\n23\t    PublicFormat,\n24\t)\n25\t\n26\tfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, Signature, TrustRoot\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass IntegrityError(Exception):\n30\t    \"\"\"Malformed key/signature material (a provisioning error, not an attacker path).\"\"\"\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _b64decode_exact(value: str, expected_len: int, what: str) -> bytes:\n34\t    try:\n35\t        raw = base64.b64decode(value, validate=True)\n36\t    except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as e:\n37\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} is not valid base64: {e}\") from e\n38\t    if len(raw) != expected_len:\n39\t        raise IntegrityError(f\"{what} decodes to {len(raw)} bytes, expected {expected_len}\")\n40\t    return raw\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef load_public_key(public_key_b64: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:\n44\t    return Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(_b64decode_exact(public_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 public key\"))\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef verify_one(public_key_b64: str, message: bytes, signature_b64: str) -> bool:\n48\t    \"\"\"True iff a valid Ed25519 signature; False on bad sig; raises only on malformed material.\"\"\"\n49\t    pub = load_public_key(public_key_b64)\n50\t    sig = _b64decode_exact(signature_b64, 64, \"Ed25519 signature\")\n51\t    try:\n52\t        pub.verify(sig, message)\n53\t        return True\n54\t    except InvalidSignature:\n55\t        return False\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n59\tclass ThresholdResult:\n60\t    satisfied: bool\n61\t    valid_signers: tuple[str, ...]\n62\t    threshold: int\n63\t    reason: str\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef verify_threshold(message: bytes, signatures: list[Signature], trust_root: TrustRoot) -> ThresholdResult:\n67\t    \"\"\"Count distinct trust-root authorisers with a valid signature; compare to threshold.\"\"\"\n68\t    by_id: dict[str, AuthorizerKey] = {a.key_id: a for a in trust_root.authorizers}\n69\t    valid: list[str] = []\n70\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n71\t    for sig in signatures:\n72\t        if sig.key_id in seen:\n73\t            continue\n74\t        seen.add(sig.key_id)\n75\t        authorizer = by_id.get(sig.key_id)\n76\t        if authorizer is None:\n77\t            continue\n78\t        if verify_one(authorizer.public_key_b64, message, sig.signature_b64):\n79\t            valid.append(sig.key_id)\n80\t    satisfied = len(valid) >= trust_root.threshold\n81\t    reason = (\n82\t        f\"{len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) >= threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n83\t        if satisfied else\n84\t        f\"only {len(valid)} valid distinct signature(s) < threshold {trust_root.threshold}\"\n85\t    )\n86\t    return ThresholdResult(satisfied=satisfied, valid_signers=tuple(valid), threshold=trust_root.threshold, reason=reason)\n87\t\n88\t\n89\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n90\tclass KeyPair:\n91\t    public_key_b64: str\n92\t    private_key_b64: str\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tdef generate_keypair() -> KeyPair:\n96\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()\n97\t    raw_priv = priv.private_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PrivateFormat.Raw, NoEncryption())\n98\t    raw_pub = priv.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)\n99\t    return KeyPair(\n100\t        public_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_pub).decode(\"ascii\"),\n101\t        private_key_b64=base64.b64encode(raw_priv).decode(\"ascii\"),\n102\t    )\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef sign(private_key_b64: str, message: bytes) -> str:\n106\t    raw = _b64decode_exact(private_key_b64, 32, \"Ed25519 private key\")\n107\t    priv = Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(raw)\n108\t    return base64.b64encode(priv.sign(message)).decode(\"ascii\")\n109\t"}]},"uuid":"9380b0e4-1f0a-4ae1-b2d8-5fd4610f9433","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:39.849Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"134b57c4-426c-417e-bb01-5d9d18750bf3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8bb99249-611f-48d3-94bf-18aa97357a09","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q82pV7YQr5WGzCscdtUWN6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Optional\n12\t\n13\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n14\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n15\t\n16\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n17\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass KillSwitch:\n21\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n22\t        self.store = store\n23\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n24\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n25\t\n26\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n27\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n28\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n29\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n30\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n31\t\n32\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n33\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n34\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n35\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n39\t        engaged = False\n40\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n41\t            p = r.payload\n42\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n43\t                continue\n44\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n45\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n46\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n47\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n48\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n49\t        return engaged\n50\t"}]},"uuid":"c8a3b9e0-7d3b-4141-935e-58556b918886","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:40.127Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8bb99249-611f-48d3-94bf-18aa97357a09","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8af647d0-d658-4a76-83df-d2438eb12752","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CgFUVbXf1DLir5WjvAeRF1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use strict\";\n2\t/* SIGIL Companion — canonical JSON. The JS<->Python PARITY CONTRACT.\n3\t *\n4\t * Byte-identical to Python (sigil/reuse/canonical.py :: canonical_json):\n5\t *     json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\",\":\"), ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n6\t * This is the #1 correctness item: the desktop bridge verifies the device signature over\n7\t * exactly these bytes (sigil/bridge/envelope.py :: envelope_message), so the phone MUST\n8\t * reproduce them or every signature is rejected.\n9\t *\n10\t * PINNED PARITY VECTOR (from tests/test_bridge_envelope.py ::\n11\t *   test_envelope_message_fixed_vector_parity):\n12\t *     core = {\"v\":1,\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000}\n13\t *     canonicalJson(core)  ===  the UTF-8 bytes of the string\n14\t *     {\"action\":\"read:snapshot\",\"args\":{},\"device\":\"DEVKEYB64\",\"nonce\":1,\"ts\":1700000000,\"v\":1}\n15\t *\n16\t * Why plain JSON.stringify is safe for the LEAVES: over the SIGIL value domain — ASCII object\n17\t * keys; strings; INTEGER numbers only (app.js floors ts to whole seconds and uses integer\n18\t * nonces / seqs — floats are NEVER signed, because float repr can diverge between the two\n19\t * runtimes); booleans; null; arrays — ECMAScript JSON.stringify emits bytes identical to Python\n20\t * json.dumps(ensure_ascii=False): the same string escapes (\\\" \\\\ \\b \\t \\n \\f \\r, all other\n21\t * control chars as lowercase \\u00xx, non-ASCII left raw, \"/\" NOT escaped), the same integer\n22\t * rendering, and the same true/false/null tokens. The ONLY two things json.dumps does that plain\n23\t * JSON.stringify does not are (1) recursive KEY SORTING and (2) the compact \",\"/\":\" separators\n24\t * with no spaces — which is precisely, and only, what this function adds.\n25\t *\n26\t * Exported for BOTH the browser (window.SigilCanonical) and Node (module.exports) so the parity\n27\t * is falsifiable: tests/test_bridge_webapp.py runs this exact function under Node and asserts the\n28\t * bytes equal Python's envelope_message for the fixed vector.\n29\t */\n30\t(function (root, factory) {\n31\t  var api = factory();\n32\t  if (typeof module !== \"undefined\" && module.exports) { module.exports = api; }   // Node\n33\t  else { root.SigilCanonical = api; }                                              // browser / worker\n34\t})(typeof self !== \"undefined\" ? self : this, function () {\n35\t  function canonicalJson(value) {\n36\t    if (value === null || typeof value !== \"object\") {\n37\t      // string / number / boolean / null — JSON.stringify matches Python for the SIGIL domain\n38\t      return JSON.stringify(value);\n39\t    }\n40\t    if (Array.isArray(value)) {\n41\t      var parts = [];\n42\t      for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) { parts.push(canonicalJson(value[i])); }\n43\t      return \"[\" + parts.join(\",\") + \"]\";\n44\t    }\n45\t    // object: sort keys (code-unit order == Unicode code-point order for ASCII keys), compact\n46\t    var keys = Object.keys(value).sort();\n47\t    var out = \"\";\n48\t    for (var j = 0; j < keys.length; j++) {\n49\t      var k = keys[j];\n50\t      if (j) { out += \",\"; }\n51\t      out += JSON.stringify(k) + \":\" + canonicalJson(value[k]);\n52\t    }\n53\t    return \"{\" + out + \"}\";\n54\t  }\n55\t  return { canonicalJson: canonicalJson };\n56\t});\n57\t"}]},"uuid":"d48f05d2-c316-4faf-95b9-c64deba480fc","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:30:44.267Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8af647d0-d658-4a76-83df-d2438eb12752","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

There is no wire\n6\t * bearer secret — authentication IS the signature over the canonical envelope core, checked against\n7\t * the owner-minted authorized-device set (sigil/mesh :: authorized_devices).\n8\t *\n9\t * CRYPTO REQUIREMENTS — WebCrypto Ed25519 is REQUIRED (no vendored/downloaded fallback: the sandbox\n10\t * is offline and we will NOT silently drop to weak crypto). Minimum browsers with Ed25519 in\n11\t * crypto.subtle:  Chrome/Edge 137+ · Safari 17+ · Firefox 129+ · Node 22 (for the parity test).\n12\t * If crypto.subtle lacks Ed25519 the app says so honestly and refuses to operate.\n13\t *\n14\t * ENCODING PARITY with sigil (sigil/reuse/crypto.py):\n15\t *   - device PUBLIC key (raw 32 bytes, exportKey(\"raw\")) and SIGNATURES (raw 64 bytes) are STANDARD\n16\t *     base64 WITH padding (b64std) — because verify_one() -> _b64decode_exact() does\n17\t *     base64.b64decode(value, validate=True), which only accepts the standard alphabet (+ / =).\n18\t *   - the WHOLE envelope JSON that rides in X-SIGIL-Envelope / ?env= is base64URL (b64url), because\n19\t *     the server decodes it with base64.urlsafe_b64decode (padding re-added server-side).\n20\t *   - canonical signing bytes come from SigilCanonical.canonicalJson (see canonical.js — the parity\n21\t *     contract). ts is floored to INTEGER seconds and nonces/seqs are integers so the JS and Python\n22\t *     canonical bytes are byte-identical (floats are never signed).\n23\t *   - the human fingerprint = grouped SHA-256(pubkey-base64-STRING) first 8 bytes as xxxx-xxxx-xxxx-\n24\t *     xxxx, matching sigil/cli.py :: _device_fingerprint (which hashes pubkey.encode(), i.e. the\n25\t *     base64 STRING, NOT the raw key bytes).\n26\t *\n27\t * CSP: default-src 'self'. No inline scripts, no inline event handlers, no external origins. All\n28\t * behaviour is wired by ONE delegated click listener + a few input handlers.\n29\t */\n30\t\n31\t/* ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- tiny DOM helpers */\n32\tvar $ = function (s) { return document.querySelector(s); };\n33\tvar esc = function (s) {\n34\t  return String(s == null ? \"\" : s).replace(/[&<>]/g, function (c) {\n35\t    return { \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\" }[c];\n36\t  });\n37\t};\n38\tvar enc = new TextEncoder();\n39\tfunction banner(kind, html) {\n40\t  var b = $(\"#banner\");\n41\t  b.className = \"banner \" + kind;\n42\t  b.innerHTML = html;\n43\t}\n44\tfunction clearBanner() { $(\"#banner\").className = \"banner info hidden\"; }\n45\t\n46\t/* ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- base64 */\n47\tfunction bytesToB64std(bytes) {                       // standard base64, WITH padding (pubkey / sig)\n48\t  var s = \"\";\n49\t  for (var i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) { s += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]); }\n50\t  return btoa(s);\n51\t}\n52\tfunction strToB64url(str) {                           // base64URL, no padding (the envelope wrapper)\n53\t  return bytesToB64std(enc.encode(str)).replace(/\\+/g, \"-\").replace(/\\//g, \"_\").replace(/=+$/g, \"\");\n54\t}\n55\tfunction toHex(buf) {\n56\t  var v = new Uint8Array(buf), out = \"\";\n57\t  for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) { out += v[i].toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"); }\n58\t  return out;\n59\t}\n60\tasync function sha256Hex(bytes) {                     // == sigil.reuse.sha256_hex\n61\t  return toHex(await crypto.subtle.digest(\"SHA-256\", bytes));\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\t/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- IndexedDB */\n65\t/* One DB, two stores: \"kv\" (device key material + the monotonic nonce) and \"outbox\" (POSTs queued\n66\t * while offline, flushed on reconnect). CryptoKey objects are structured-cloneable, so the\n67\t * NON-EXTRACTABLE private key persists here without ever exposing its bytes. */\n68\tvar DB = null;\n69\tfunction openDB() {\n70\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n71\t    var req = indexedDB.open(\"sigil-companion\", 1);\n72\t    req.onupgradeneeded = function () {\n73\t      var db = req.result;\n74\t      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(\"kv\")) { db.createObjectStore(\"kv\", { keyPath: \"k\" }); }\n75\t      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(\"outbox\")) { db.createObjectStore(\"outbox\", { keyPath: \"id\", autoIncrement: true }); }\n76\t    };\n77\t    req.onsuccess = function () { resolve(req.result); };\n78\t    req.onerror = function () { reject(req.error); };\n79\t  });\n80\t}\n81\tfunction tx(store, mode) { return DB.transaction(store, mode).objectStore(store); }\n82\tfunction idbGet(store, key) {\n83\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n84\t    var r = tx(store, \"readonly\").get(key);\n85\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(r.result); };\n86\t    r.onerror = function () { reject(r.error); };\n87\t  });\n88\t}\n89\tfunction idbPut(store, val) {\n90\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n91\t    var r = tx(store, \"readwrite\").put(val);\n92\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(r.result); };\n93\t    r.onerror = function () { reject(r.error); };\n94\t  });\n95\t}\n96\tfunction idbDel(store, key) {\n97\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n98\t    var r = tx(store, \"readwrite\").delete(key);\n99\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(); };\n100\t    r.onerror = function () { reject(r.error); };\n101\t  });\n102\t}\n103\tfunction idbAll(store) {\n104\t  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {\n105\t    var r = tx(store, \"readonly\").getAll();\n106\t    r.onsuccess = function () { resolve(r.result || []); };\n107\t    r.onerror = function () { reject(r.error); };\n108\t  });\n109\t}\n110\t\n111\t/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------ device key + identity */\n112\tvar DEVICE = null;   // { privateKey: CryptoKey (non-extractable), pubB64: string, deviceId: string, fingerprint: string }\n113\t\n114\tasync function ed25519Available() {\n115\t  if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle && crypto.subtle.generateKey)) { return false; }\n116\t  try {\n117\t    var k = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: \"Ed25519\" }, false, [\"sign\", \"verify\"]);\n118\t    return !!(k && k.privateKey && k.publicKey);\n119\t  } catch (e) { return false; }\n120\t}\n121\t\n122\tfunction randomDeviceId() {\n123\t  var b = new Uint8Array(4);\n124\t  crypto.getRandomValues(b);\n125\t  return \"phone-\" + toHex(b.buffer);\n126\t}\n127\t\n128\tasync function loadOrCreateDevice() {\n129\t  var rec = await idbGet(\"kv\", \"device\");\n130\t  if (rec && rec.privateKey && rec.pubB64) {\n131\t    DEVICE = { privateKey: rec.privateKey, pubB64: rec.pubB64, deviceId: rec.deviceId, fingerprint: rec.fingerprint };\n132\t    return DEVICE;\n133\t  }\n134\t  // First run on this device: mint a fresh NON-EXTRACTABLE key. [\"sign\",\"verify\"] so the (always-\n135\t  // extractable) public key carries a valid usage; the private key stays extractable=false. We only\n136\t  // ever exportKey(\"raw\") the PUBLIC key.\n137\t  var pair = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: \"Ed25519\" }, false, [\"sign\", \"verify\"]);\n138\t  var raw = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey(\"raw\", pair.publicKey));  // 32 bytes\n139\t  var pubB64 = bytesToB64std(raw);\n140\t  var short = (await sha256Hex(enc.encode(pubB64))).slice(0, 16);\n141\t  var fingerprint = [short.slice(0, 4), short.slice(4, 8), short.slice(8, 12), short.slice(12, 16)].join(\"-\");\n142\t  var deviceId = randomDeviceId();\n143\t  DEVICE = { privateKey: pair.privateKey, pubB64: pubB64, deviceId: deviceId, fingerprint: fingerprint };\n144\t  await idbPut(\"kv\", { k: \"device\", privateKey: pair.privateKey, pubB64: pubB64, deviceId: deviceId, fingerprint: fingerprint });\n145\t  await idbPut(\"kv\", { k: \"nonce\", v: 0 });\n146\t  return DEVICE;\n147\t}\n148\t\n149\tasync function nextNonce() {                          // monotonic, per-device, persisted (replay gate)\n150\t  var rec = await idbGet(\"kv\", \"nonce\");\n151\t  var n = (rec && typeof rec.v === \"number\" ? rec.v : 0) + 1;\n152\t  await idbPut(\"kv\", { k: \"nonce\", v: n });\n153\t  return n;\n154\t}\n155\t\n156\tasync function signBytes(msgBytes) {                  // -> standard-base64 signature\n157\t  var sig = await crypto.subtle.sign(\"Ed25519\", DEVICE.privateKey, msgBytes);\n158\t  return bytesToB64std(new Uint8Array(sig));\n159\t}\n160\t\n161\t/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------- envelope builder */\n162\t/* core = {v,device,action,args,nonce,ts}; sign canonicalJson(core); wrapper = base64url(JSON). */\n163\tasync function buildEnvelope(action, args, nonce) {\n164\t  var core = {\n165\t    v: 1,\n166\t    device: DEVICE.pubB64,\n167\t    action: action,\n168\t    args: args || {},\n169\t    nonce: nonce,\n170\t    ts: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)   // INTEGER seconds — byte-parity with Python; server window is +/-120s\n171\t  };\n172\t  var sig = await signBytes(enc.encode(SigilCanonical.canonicalJson(core)));\n173\t  var wrapper = {};\n174\t  for (var kk in core) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(core, kk)) { wrapper[kk] = core[kk]; } }\n175\t  wrapper.sig = sig;\n176\t  return strToB64url(JSON.stringify(wrapper));\n177\t}\n178\t\n179\t/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- networking */\n180\tfunction on401(status) {                              // a read/action was refused as unauthenticated\n181\t  if (status === 401 || status === 403) {\n182\t    banner(\"warn\", \"This device is not authorized yet (or its signature was refused). Pair it on the PC \"\n183\t      + \"(see the Pairing panel), then it will connect.\");\n184\t  }\n185\t}\n186\t\n187\tasync function authedGet(path, action, extraQuery) {  // a signed READ (envelope in the header)\n188\t  var env = await buildEnvelope(action, {}, await nextNonce());\n189\t  var url = path + (extraQuery ? (\"?\" + extraQuery) : \"\");\n190\t  var r = await fetch(url, { headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env }, cache: \"no-store\" });\n191\t  if (!r.ok) { on401(r.status); throw new Error(\"GET \" + path + \" -> \" + r.status); }\n192\t  clearBanner();\n193\t  return r.json();\n194\t}\n195\t\n196\tasync function effectfulPost(path, action, args) {    // panic / relay — signed, replay-gated\n197\t  var env = await buildEnvelope(action, args || {}, await nextNonce());\n198\t  var req = { path: path, headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Envelope\": env }, body: \"\" };\n199\t  return deliver(req);\n200\t}\n201\t\n202\tasync function postApproval(seq, decision) {          // device-signed governor.approval -> /api/action\n203\t  var approver = \"device\";\n204\t  var msgBytes = enc.encode(SigilCanonical.canonicalJson({ approver: approver, decision: decision, target: seq }));\n205\t  var sig = await signBytes(msgBytes);\n206\t  var payload = {\n207\t    signal: \"governor.approval\", approval: decision, target_seq: seq,\n208\t    approver: approver, reason: \"\", pubkey: DEVICE.pubB64, sig: sig,\n209\t    msg_digest: await sha256Hex(msgBytes), device: true\n210\t  };\n211\t  var req = { path: \"/api/action\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) };\n212\t  return deliver(req);\n213\t}\n214\t\n215\t/* deliver: try now; if the network is unreachable, QUEUE it (already signed) and flush on reconnect.\n216\t * If the server RESPONDS (any HTTP status) the item is considered delivered — the server adjudicated. */\n217\tasync function deliver(req) {\n218\t  try {\n219\t    var r = await fetch(req.path, {\n220\t      method: \"POST\", headers: req.headers, body: req.body, cache: \"no-store\"\n221\t    });\n222\t    var j = await r.json().catch(function () { return {}; will flush on reconnect\" } };\n227\t  }\n228\t}\n229\t\n230\tasync function flushOutbox() {\n231\t  if (!DB) { return; }\n232\t  var items = await idbAll(\"outbox\");\n233\t  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {\n234\t    var it = items[i];\n235\t    try {\n236\t      await fetch(it.req.path, { method: \"POST\", headers: it.req.headers, body: it.req.body, cache: \"no-store\" });\n237\t      await idbDel(\"outbox\", it.id);                 // server responded (any status) -> stop retrying\n238\t    } catch (e) {\n239\t      /* still offline — keep it queued */\n240\t    }\n241\t  }\n242\t  renderOutbox();\n243\t}\n244\t\n245\t/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- renderers */\n246\tasync function renderPairing() {\n247\t  var host = $(\"#pair-body\");\n248\t  host.innerHTML =\n249\t    '<div class=\"mono\">device id</div><code class=\"sel\">' + esc(DEVICE.deviceId) + '</code>'\n250\t    + '<div class=\"mono mt\">device public key (base64)</div><code class=\"sel\" id=\"pubkey\">' + esc(DEVICE.pubB64) + '</code>'\n251\t    + '<div class=\"mono mt\">fingerprint (eyeball-match this on the PC)</div><div class=\"fp\">' + esc(DEVICE.fingerprint) + '</div>'\n252\t    + '<div class=\"acts mt\"><button data-act=\"copy-pub\">Copy public key</button>'\n253\t    + '<button data-act=\"copy-cmd\">Copy authorize command</button></div>'\n254\t    + '<div class=\"instr\">On the PC, run:<br><code id=\"authcmd\">sigil mesh authorize ' + esc(DEVICE.deviceId) + ' ' + esc(DEVICE.pubB64)\n255\t    + '</code><br>Confirm the printed fingerprint equals <b>' + esc(DEVICE.fingerprint) + '</b> before you accept it.</div>';\n256\t}\n257\t\n258\tasync function refreshPending() {\n259\t  var q = $(\"#queue\");\n260\t  var data;\n261\t  try { data = await authedGet(\"/api/pending\", \"read:pending\"); ' + esc(v.interrupts) + ' interrupts</span>';\n292\t  }).join(\"\") || '<span class=\"empty\">none</span>';\n293\t  $(\"#activity\").innerHTML = kv(s.recent_by_agent) || '<span class=\"empty\">quiet</span>';\n294\t  var l = s.ingest_lag || {};\n295\t  $(\"#lag\").innerHTML = '<b>head</b><span>' + esc(l.head_seq) + '</span>'\n296\t    + '<b>since checkpoint</b><span>' + esc(l.records_since_checkpoint) + '</span>';\n297\t}\n298\t\n299\tasync function showRecord(seq) {\n300\t  var r;\n301\t  try { r = await authedGet(\"/api/record/\" + seq, \"read:record\"); Behaviour is byte-identical.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom typing import Any, Final\n\n# Never change without a schema bump + migration (invalidates all existing signatures).\n_EVIDENCE_DOMAIN: Final[bytes] = b\"crucible-evidence-v1\\x00\"\n\n\ndef canonical_json(payload: Any) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Deterministic UTF-8 JSON: sorted keys (recursive), compact separators.\"\"\"\n    return json.dumps(\n        payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(\",\", \":\"), ensure_ascii=False,\n    ).encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef sha256_hex(data: bytes) -> str:\n    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef digest_payload(payload: Any) -> str:\n    \"\"\"sha256 of a JSON-serialisable payload in canonical form.\"\"\"\n    return sha256_hex(canonical_json(payload))\n\n\ndef evidence_signing_bytes(certificate_payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified (canonical JSON under the domain tag).\"\"\"\n    return _EVIDENCE_DOMAIN + canonical_json(certificate_payload)\n=== reuse __init__ ===\n\"\"\"SIGIL's vendored integrity substrate (owner's own CRUCIBLE work, copied verbatim).\n\nThe one seam that would ever couple to the engine — now fully self-contained. SIGIL\nimports NO `framework.*` module at all, which is the strongest form of the sovereignty\ndoctrine (§1.3 / §12): the offensive engine cannot even be loaded into a SIGIL process.\n`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nfrom .canonical import canonical_json, digest_payload, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\nfrom .chain import append_entry, build_chain, sign_head, verify_chain, verify_head\nfrom .crypto import generate_keypair, sign, verify_one, verify_threshold, KeyPair\nfrom .models import AuthorizerKey, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"canonical_json\", \"digest_payload\", \"evidence_signing_bytes\", \"sha256_hex\",\n    \"append_entry\", \"build_chain\", \"sign_head\", \"verify_chain\", \"verify_head\",\n    \"generate_keypair\", \"sign\", \"verify_one\", \"verify_threshold\", \"KeyPair\",\n    \"AuthorizerKey\", \"ChainEntry\", \"Signature\", \"SignedChainHead\", \"TrustRoot\",\n    \"assert_no_offense\",\n]\n\n\ndef assert_no_offense() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Fail-closed: NO framework.* module may be loaded in a SIGIL process (doctrine §12).\"\"\"\n    leaked = [m for m in sys.modules if m == \"framework\" or m.startswith(\"framework.\")]\n    if leaked:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"SIGIL sovereignty violation: engine modules loaded: {leaked}\")\n=== mesh __init__ ===\n\"\"\"SIGIL mesh (Phase 7, WS-D D-iii/iv) — \"distributed compute, centralized authority\". Host\ncapabilities and phone/device authorizations are owner-SIGNED spine records, so the mesh topology\nis itself provable, tamper-evident memory; compute (vision/VLM) can run on the best-capable online\nhost, but authority still flows through the one signed spine + WARDEN. {"parentUuid":"668db249-496d-4e86-ae6b-98193bb3df76","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c7c0b4cd-e6c6-4488-a68b-ca4f4169315f","agentId":"afa44d52b18cbaba0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JLCHmVSzaEBjSSLzx7nNGT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL Phase 9 W2-K — device-signed REMOTE ARM of a gesture session (the deliberate, reviewed\n2\ttrust-widening). Proves the full threat table: only an owner-authorized device can arm; forged /\n3\treplayed / stale / after-revoke arms are refused; the TTL is clamped shorter than a local arm; a device\n4\tarm never displaces a live session; the owner's kill-switch/disarm always wins; and every downstream\n5\tbound (A1-inject / A2-queue) is UNCHANGED for a device-armed session.\n6\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python tests/test_gesture_arm.py\"\"\"\n7\timport tempfile\n8\timport time\n9\t\n10\tfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\n11\tfrom sigil.bridge.daemon import BridgeDaemon\n12\tfrom sigil.gesture.components import RecordingInputBackend\n13\tfrom sigil.gesture.session import MAX_DEVICE_TTL, SESSION_ARMED, SessionGate, arm_request_message, sign_arm_request\n14\tfrom sigil.gesture.types import GestureIntent\n15\tfrom sigil.mesh import authorize_device, revoke_device\n16\tfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair, verify_one\n17\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n18\t\n19\tOWNER = generate_keypair()\n20\tOP = OWNER.public_key_b64\n21\tDEV = generate_keypair()          # a phone the owner will authorize\n22\tATTACKER = generate_keypair()     # a device the owner NEVER authorized\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef _store():\n26\t    return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass FakeCls:\n30\t    \"\"\"hid.pointer.* → A1 (inject in-session); hid.type/combo/launch → A2 (queue). No kernel subprocess.\"\"\"\n31\t    def classify(self, tool):\n32\t        return Tier.A1 if tool.startswith(\"hid.pointer\") else Tier.A2\n33\t\n34\t\n35\tdef _gate(store, backend=None):\n36\t    return SessionGate(store, backend or RecordingInputBackend(), classifier=FakeCls(), trusted_pubkey=OP)\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tdef _authorize(store, kp=DEV, device_id=\"phone1\"):\n40\t    authorize_device(store, device_id, kp.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef _req(kp=DEV, *, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=None, ttl=120.0):\n44\t    return sign_arm_request(kp, device_id=device_id, nonce=nonce, ts=ts if ts is not None else time.time(), ttl_seconds=ttl)\n45\t\n46\t\n47\t# ---- happy path + TTL clamp ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n48\tdef test_authorized_device_arms_and_ttl_is_clamped():\n49\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n50\t    now = time.time()\n51\t    sess = g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=120.0), now=now)\n52\t    assert sess is not None and sess.live, \"an authorized, fresh, signed request arms a session\"\n53\t    assert abs(sess.expires_at - (now + 120.0)) < 1e-6, \"ttl within MAX is honored\"\n54\t    # a request over the cap clamps to MAX_DEVICE_TTL (a trust-narrowing inside the widening)\n55\t    s2 = _store(); _authorize(s2); g2 = _gate(s2)\n56\t    sess2 = g2.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now, ttl=99999.0), now=now)\n57\t    assert abs(sess2.expires_at - (now + MAX_DEVICE_TTL)) < 1e-6, f\"ttl clamped to {MAX_DEVICE_TTL}\"\n58\t\n59\t\n60\t# ---- the threat table (each refusal records a refusal + returns None + arms nothing) --------------\n61\tdef test_forged_device_cannot_arm():\n62\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)                    # DEV authorized, ATTACKER is not\n63\t    g = _gate(s)\n64\t    now = time.time()\n65\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(kp=ATTACKER, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"an unauthorized device cannot arm\"\n66\t    assert g.session is None\n67\t    assert any(r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"gesture.arm_request\" and r.payload.get(\"decision\") == \"refused\"\n68\t               for r in s.iter_records()), \"the refusal is recorded\"\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tdef test_tampered_signature_refused():\n72\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n73\t    now = time.time()\n74\t    req = _req(ts=now)\n75\t    req[\"ttl_seconds\"] = 999.0                      # mutate a SIGNED field after signing → sig no longer matches\n76\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is None and g.session is None, \"a tampered arm request is refused\"\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef test_replayed_arm_nonce_refused():\n80\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n81\t    now = time.time()\n82\t    req = _req(nonce=7, ts=now)\n83\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is not None, \"first arm with nonce 7 succeeds\"\n84\t    g.disarm()\n85\t    assert g.arm_by_device(req, now=now) is None, \"re-submitting the SAME (device,nonce) is refused (replay)\"\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef test_stale_arm_refused():\n89\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n90\t    now = time.time()\n91\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=now - 100.0), now=now) is None, \"a stale (old-ts) arm request is refused\"\n92\t\n93\t\n94\tdef test_arm_after_revoke_refused():\n95\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n96\t    now = time.time()\n97\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now), now=now) is not None, \"authorized device arms\"\n98\t    g.disarm()\n99\t    revoke_device(s, \"phone1\", DEV.public_key_b64, OWNER)\n100\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=2, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"a REVOKED device can no longer arm\"\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tdef test_killswitch_blocks_arm():\n104\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n105\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).panic_engage(by=\"test\")     # owner/phone panic\n106\t    now = time.time()\n107\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=now), now=now) is None, \"no arm while the kill-switch is engaged\"\n108\t\n109\t\n110\tdef test_single_session_not_displaced_by_device_arm():\n111\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n112\t    now = time.time()\n113\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=1, ts=now), now=now) is not None, \"first arm succeeds\"\n114\t    assert g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=2, ts=now), now=now) is None, \"a device arm never displaces a live session\"\n115\t\n116\t\n117\t# ---- owner always wins + downstream bounds unchanged ---------------------------------------------\n118\tdef test_owner_disarm_beats_a_device_armed_session():\n119\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n120\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n121\t    g.disarm()\n122\t    v = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n123\t    assert not v[\"injected\"] and b.calls == [], \"after owner disarm, a device-armed session injects nothing\"\n124\t\n125\t\n126\tdef test_killswitch_neuters_a_device_armed_session_mid_flight():\n127\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n128\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n129\t    BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP).panic_engage(by=\"test\")     # owner panic AFTER arming\n130\t    v = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n131\t    assert not v[\"injected\"] and \"kill-switch\" in v[\"reason\"], \"an owner panic neuters injection mid-session\"\n132\t    assert b.calls == [] and g.session is None, \"the session is disarmed on the kill-switch\"\n133\t\n134\t\n135\tdef test_device_armed_session_still_bounds_to_a1_and_queues_type():\n136\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); b = RecordingInputBackend(); g = _gate(s, b)\n137\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(ts=time.time()), now=time.time())\n138\t    mv = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"move\", dx=0.5, dy=0.5))\n139\t    assert mv[\"injected\"] and mv[\"tier\"] == \"A1\", \"a pointer move auto-injects (A1) in a device-armed session\"\n140\t    ty = g.handle(GestureIntent(\"type\", arg=\"secret\"))\n141\t    assert not ty[\"injected\"] and ty.get(\"queued\") is not None, \"type NEVER auto-injects — it QUEUES (A2), even device-armed\"\n142\t\n143\t\n144\t# ---- the arm record is self-verifying + the daemon transport gate ---------------------------------\n145\tdef test_arm_record_is_self_verifying_against_the_device_ledger():\n146\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s); g = _gate(s)\n147\t    now = time.time()\n148\t    g.arm_by_device(_req(nonce=42, ts=now, ttl=120.0), now=now)\n149\t    rec = next(r for r in s.iter_records()\n150\t               if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and r.payload.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\")\n151\t    p = rec.payload\n152\t    core = {\"signal\": \"gesture.arm_request\", \"device_id\": p[\"device_id\"], \"nonce\": p[\"nonce\"],\n153\t            \"ts\": p[\"ts\"], \"ttl_seconds\": p[\"ttl_seconds\"]}\n154\t    assert verify_one(p[\"device_pubkey\"], arm_request_message(core), p[\"sig\"]), \\\n155\t        \"the armed record's authorization re-verifies against the device signature (tamper-evident)\"\n156\t\n157\t\n158\tdef test_daemon_submit_arm_request_gates_on_authorization():\n159\t    s = _store(); _authorize(s)\n160\t    d = BridgeDaemon(s, trusted_pubkey=OP)\n161\t    now = time.time()\n162\t    seq = d.submit_arm_request(_req(ts=now))\n163\t    assert isinstance(seq, int), \"an authorized, signed arm request is recorded\"\n164\t    try:\n165\t        d.submit_arm_request(_req(kp=ATTACKER, ts=now))\n166\t        assert False, \"an unauthorized device's arm request must be refused\"\n167\t    except ValueError:\n168\t        pass\n169\t    bad = _req(ts=now); bad[\"sig\"] = \"AAAA\"          # forged/garbage signature\n170\t    try:\n171\t        d.submit_arm_request(bad)\n172\t        assert False, \"a bad signature must be refused\"\n173\t    except ValueError:\n174\t        pass\n175\t\n176\t\n177\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n178\t    fns = [v for k, v in sorted(globals().items()) if k.startswith(\"test_\")]\n179\t    passed = 0\n180\t    for fn in fns:\n181\t        try:\n182\t            fn(); Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\nsigil/agents/kernel_classify.py:31:            return Tier.A3\nsigil/agents/kernel_classify.py:36:            return Tier.A3\nsigil/agents/kernel_classify.py:38:            return Tier.A3\nsigil/agents/kernel_classify.py:41:            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\nsigil/agents/kernel_classify.py:43:            return Tier.A3\n=== find classify ===\nactor_gate.py\nactor.py\nactor_scope.py\napprovals.py\narchivist.py\nartificer.py\nbase.py\nbastion.py\nenvoy.py\n__init__.py\n---\n\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\nclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\ncomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\nTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\nfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\ntiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n\nConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\nrecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\ntier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\nclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\n\nfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\nfrom .base import Tier\n\n_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n\n\nclass KernelClassifier:\n    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n        self.timeout = timeout\n\n    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n            return Tier.A3\n        try:\n            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n            return Tier.A3\n        if proc.returncode != 0:\n            return Tier.A3\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n            return Tier.A3","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"89102a36-4acb-4a57-88e9-4e108f3ad57b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:11.530Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e6baa01f-6437-458c-99e4-78d8b0193169","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

OP = OWNER.public_key_b64\nDEV = generate_keypair()\n\nclass FakeCls:\n    def classify(self, tool): return Tier.A1 if tool.startswith(\"hid.pointer\") else Tier.A2\n\ndef store(): return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\ndef gate(s,b=None): return SessionGate(s,b or RecordingInputBackend(),classifier=FakeCls(),trusted_pubkey=OP)\n\nprint(\"=== FINDING A: NaN ttl_seconds -> unbounded device-armed session (defeats MAX_DEVICE_TTL clamp) ===\")\ns=store(); authorize_device(s2,\"phone1\",DEV.public_key_b64,OWNER); g2=gate(s2)\nreq2 = sign_arm_request(DEV, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=float(\"nan\"), ttl_seconds=120.0)\nsess2 = g2.arm_by_device(req2, now=now)\nprint(\"NaN-ts arm accepted (freshness bypassed)?\", sess2 is not None, \"-> abs(now-nan) > 30 is\", abs(now-float('nan'))>30.0)\n\nprint()\nprint(\"=== FINDING A3: +Inf ttl is correctly clamped (control) ===\")\ns3=store(); authorize_device(s3,\"phone1\",DEV.public_key_b64,OWNER); g3=gate(s3)\nreq3 = sign_arm_request(DEV, device_id=\"phone1\", nonce=1, ts=now, ttl_seconds=float(\"inf\"))\nsess3 = g3.arm_by_device(req3, now=now)\nprint(\"Inf ttl -> expires_at =\", sess3.expires_at, \"== now+MAX?\", abs(sess3.expires_at-(now+MAX_DEVICE_TTL))<1e-6)\nPY\n~/.sigil/venv/bin/python /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_nan.py","description":"Repro NaN ttl/ts arm bypass"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2158,"cache_read_input_tokens":97283,"output_tokens":1754,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2158},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1754,"cache_read_input_tokens":97283,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2158,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2158,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUdfNSRbUPFMFW3i7Aa","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"8ee1c741-abab-49df-b9a5-b3d65a77ca8f","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:37.383Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

reconstruct from the RECORD's own fields\n    ok = verify_one(p[\"device_pubkey\"], arm_request_message(core), p[\"sig\"])\n    print(f\"{label}: requested ttl={ttl_req}, stored ttl_seconds={p['ttl_seconds']}, record re-verifies from its OWN fields? {ok}\")\n\nprint(\"=== FINDING B: the device-armed SESSION_ARMED record is NOT self-verifying when ttl is clamped ===\")\ncheck(120.0, \"unclamped (test's chosen value)\")\ncheck(99999.0, \"CLAMPED (ttl>MAX_DEVICE_TTL)\")\ncheck(None,   \"ttl absent (signed=None, stored=clamped 300)\")\nPY\n~/.sigil/venv/bin/python /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/repro_selfverify.py","description":"Repro clamped-ttl record self-verification break"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2079,"cache_read_input_tokens":99441,"output_tokens":1333,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2079},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1333,"cache_read_input_tokens":99441,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2079,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2079,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAUfj6UsYSKq3uewTyir","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"6a4ca3c2-c976-4ab2-82cf-ba0dc728f6ee","timestamp":"2026-07-19T00:34:57.939Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
